			node("inner-work", "mutate", 600),
			gate("inner-done", 900),
			gate("outer-done", 1200),

					}]

			edge("choose-finish", "choose", "outer-done", "finish"),
			edge("inner-return", "inner-done", "inner-work", "repeat"),
			edge("outer-return", "outer-done", "outer-work", "repeat")
		]

 ? src/editor/routing.test.ts > backward routing geometry > function decisionBackRoute() {
	return {
		schemaVersion: __vite_ssr_import_0__.CURRENT_DEFINITION_FORMAT,
		name: "Decision back-route",
		nodes: [
			node("start", "trigger", 0),
			node("work", "mutate", 300),
			{

				kind: "decision",
				config: {
					kind: "expression",
					jsonata: "\"done\"",
					routes: [
						{
							id: "retry",
							label: "Retry"
						},
						{
							id: "again",
							label: "Again"
						},
						{
							id: "done",
							label: "Done"
						}
					]
				}
			},
			node("done", "exit", 900)
		],
		edges: [
			edge("start-work", "start", "work"),
			edge("work-decide", "work", "decide"),
			edge("retry", "decide", "work", "retry"),
			edge("self", "decide", "decide", "again"),
			edge("finish", "decide", "done", "done")
		]
	};
}: orthogonal, padded, distinct lanes and straight labels 15ms
 ? src/editor/routing.test.ts > backward routing geometry > function denseGraph(count = 100) {
	const nodes = [node("start", "trigger", 0, 0)];
	const edges = [edge("begin", "start", "n1")];
	for (let i = 1; i < count - 1; i += 1) {
		const labels = i < 3 ? [
			"next",
			"retry",
			"self"
		] : ["next", "retry"];
		nodes.push({
			...node(`n${i}`, "decision", i % 10 * 320, Math.floor(i / 10) * 270),
			kind: "decision",
			config: {
				kind: "expression",
				jsonata: "\"next\"",
				routes: labels.map((id) => ({
					id,
					label: id
				}))
			}
		});
		edges.push(edge(`next-${i}`, `n${i}`, i === count - 2 ? "done" : `n${i + 1}`, "next"));
		edges.push(edge(`retry-${i}`, `n${i}`, i === 1 ? "n1" : `n${i - 1}`, "retry"));
		if (i < 3) edges.push(edge(`self-${i}`, `n${i}`, `n${i}`, "self"));
	};
	nodes.push(gate("done", 9 * 320, Math.floor((count - 1) / 10) * 270));
	edges.push(edge("return", "done", "n1", "repeat"));
	return {
		schemaVersion: __vite_ssr_import_0__.CURRENT_DEFINITION_FORMAT,
		name: `Dense graph: ${count} nodes, ${count * 2} edges`,
		nodes,
		edges
	};
}: orthogonal, padded, distinct lanes and straight labels 847ms
